    If White plays `c2c1`, it's a defensive move.

    If White is on turn, and the only legal moves are those listed, then any of them is valid.
    `f4f5` looks like a standard attempt to push pawns.

    Wait, look at the Black King at c8, Black Queen at b7, Rooks at d8 and h8, Knight at g8.
    Black has a huge material lead. White is essentially lost, but `f4f5` is as good as any.
